ZANZAN CASH SERVICE MIGRATION — ACCOUNTING MODULE

============================================================
1. Project Identity
============================================================

This document describes the Accounting Module, a General-Ledger (GL) ERP
extension built inside the Zanzan Cash Service Migration project.

The parent project (see /CLAUDE.md and docs/gates/kickoff-contract.md at the
repository root) is a Gate-driven migration of the WordPress plugin
`zanzan-simple-cash-saas` (v1.8.33) into a standalone, Google-Cloud-deployable
Cash Service. That migration's own Cash Module reached a frozen v1.0 baseline
on `main`.

The Accounting Module is a SEPARATE, later engineering effort layered on top
of that frozen Cash Module, developed entirely on the branch
`feature/accounting-module`. It is not part of the original Gate 1-5 Kickoff
scope; it was proposed, planned, and Gate-Reviewed as its own sequence of
versions (v0.1 through v0.4, each independently PASSed) through the project's
established Slack Gate Review pipeline (`#ai-gate-test`).

This handoff document exists so that a reader with ONLY this text file (no
GitHub access, no Slack history, no prior chat context) can understand:
  - what the Accounting Module is and how it works today,
  - what state each version reached,
  - what is and is not implemented,
  - and can read the actual full source of every relevant file.

Every fact in this document is derived from reading the actual files in the
repository at the commit recorded in Section 3, not from memory of past
conversations. Where anything here would conflict with the actual current
code, the code is authoritative and any such discrepancy is flagged
explicitly (see Section 7 and Section 17).
============================================================

action has ever been taken for this branch. Nothing in this handoff document
authorizes starting v0.5 or merging to `main` — see Section 19.

============================================================
4. Project Goal
============================================================

Parent project goal (for context): migrate `zanzan-simple-cash-saas`, a
WordPress plugin, into a standalone Cash Service deployable to Google Cloud,
ending at a staging/non-production deployment (see /CLAUDE.md and
docs/gates/kickoff-contract.md). The parent project's Gate 1-5 sequence is
separate from, and already completed/frozen ahead of, the Accounting Module
work described in this document.

Accounting Module's own goal (this document's scope): build a General-
Ledger-native ERP extension on top of the frozen Cash Module, and, through
that extension, formalize a Journal provenance contract that Cash and future
AP/AR/FA/Payroll/System modules can all share. In the project's own stated
framing:

  建立 GL 原生過帳入口，並正式建立未來 Cash/AP/AR/FA/Payroll/System
  共用的 Journal provenance contract
  (Establish a GL-native posting entry point, and formally establish the
  Journal provenance contract to be shared in future by Cash and by the
  future AP/AR/FA/Payroll/System modules.)

Concretely, this means:
  - The Accounting Module reads Cash Module data (accounts, categories,
    cash entries) to classify and turn it into journal entries, but never
    writes to or mutates anything in the Cash Module.
  - Every JournalEntry, regardless of which module produced it, carries a
    uniform provenance (`sourceType` / `sourceModule` / `sourceReferenceId`)
    — established for real in v0.4, with Cash Module postings retrofitted
    onto the same contract.
  - A human-operated GL-native posting entry point (Manual Journal Entry /
    General Voucher, v0.4) now exists alongside the existing Cash-derived
    automatic posting path, writing into the exact same GL tables — proving
    the "one GL, multiple posting paths" architecture that future AP/AR/FA/
    Payroll/System modules are meant to reuse rather than reinvent.

No new product features beyond this GL/ERP scope were built — no OCR, no AI
bookkeeping, no mobile app, no tax engine, no multi-currency, consistent
with the parent project's own "no new product features" posture.
============================================================
5. Architecture Overview
============================================================

Layering (mirrors the Cash Module's own layering exactly, applied to a
fully separate set of files/state):

  backend/src/domain/accounting/**                  domain types, errors,
                                                      repository interfaces,
                                                      services (pure business
                                                      logic; framework-free)
  backend/src/infra/memory/accounting/**             in-memory repository
                                                      implementations +
                                                      AccountingDatabase +
                                                      AccountingUnitOfWork
  frontend/src/localdb/accounting/**                 browser-localStorage-
                                                      backed persistence that
                                                      reuses the SAME backend
                                                      domain/service classes
                                                      directly (no HTTP layer
                                                      exists for this module
                                                      yet — see Section 17)
  frontend/src/features/accounting/**                React UI components

Fully separate from the Cash Module at every layer:
  - own domain folder (backend/src/domain/accounting/), never imports from
    backend/src/domain/ outside that folder except by reading Cash Module
    shapes structurally (CashEntryLike in LedgerMappingService.ts)
  - own error type (AccountingError, not Cash Module's DomainError)
  - own id generator (generateAccountingId, independent counter)
  - own in-memory store (AccountingDatabase, never shares a Map with Cash
    Module's InMemoryDatabase)
  - own frontend localStorage key (`zzcs_accounting_db_v1`), never the Cash
    Module's `zzcs_prototype_db_v1`

HARD BOUNDARY: the Accounting Module is a CONSUMER of the Cash Module, NEVER
A MUTATOR of it. It reads Cash Module data through the Cash Module's own
already-initialized services (`CashEntryService.listEntries`,
`AccountService.listForAdmin`, `CategoryService.listForAdmin`, etc.) but
never calls any Cash Module method that writes. This is enforced by
convention (there is no technical barrier preventing a write call — the
Accounting Module code simply never makes one) and is verified by dedicated
tests in every version's test suite that snapshot Cash Module data before
and after every Accounting Module operation and assert byte-for-byte
equality.

There is only ONE general ledger. Both the Cash-derived automatic posting
path and the v0.4 manual General Voucher posting path write into the exact
same JournalEntry / JournalLine / Voucher repositories and tables — there is
no second ledger, no parallel voucher table, no separate "manual GL" store.
This is the central architectural claim of v0.4 and is proven directly by
tests (see Section 8).
============================================================
6. Module / Data Flow
============================================================

Two posting paths converge on the same GL:

  CASH-DERIVED PATH (v0.1, still the primary path today)
  --------------------------------------------------------
  CashEntry (Cash Module)
    -> LedgerMappingService.classify()            [mapped / unmapped / excluded]
    -> JournalEntryService.journalizeEntry()       (single entry) or
       JournalEntryService.processPending()        (batch, from the Dashboard's
                                                      "產生分錄" button)
    -> creates JournalEntry (sourceType='module', sourceModule='CASH')
       + 2 balanced JournalLines
    -> VoucherService.createForJournalEntry()      -> 1 Voucher (1:1 with the
                                                        JournalEntry)
    -> feeds Trial Balance / Income Statement / Balance Sheet

  MANUAL GENERAL VOUCHER PATH (v0.4, new)
  --------------------------------------------------------
  GeneralVoucherDraft (freely editable scratchpad; status='draft')
    -> GeneralVoucherService.postDraft()           [the ONLY path that ever
                                                      turns a draft into GL data]
       - runs fully inside AccountingUnitOfWork.runAtomic()
       - validates: >=2 lines -> line well-formedness -> every line's
         ChartOfAccount tenant-owned+active -> SigmaDebit=SigmaCredit ->
         entryDate not in a closed FiscalPeriod
    -> creates JournalEntry (sourceType='manual', sourceModule='GL')
       + N balanced JournalLines                    [SAME JournalEntry table]
    -> VoucherService.createForJournalEntry()       -> 1 Voucher            [SAME Voucher table]
    -> draft updated: status='posted', postedJournalEntryId, postedVoucherId,
       postedAt (all four mutations atomic together)
    -> feeds Trial Balance / Income Statement / Balance Sheet identically to
       a Cash-derived entry

Both paths' output (JournalEntry/JournalLine/Voucher) is read, read-only, by
three financial-statement services (TrialBalanceService,
IncomeStatementService, BalanceSheetService) and by FiscalPeriodService's
posting-lock check — none of these ever distinguish a Cash-derived entry
from a manual entry when computing a report; they only ever read
JournalEntry/JournalLine data uniformly, using the provenance fields (if at
all) purely for display (e.g. the "來源" column in the UI), never for
different report math.

A GeneralVoucherDraft, while status='draft', is invisible to all three
statements — they never read the draft table at all, only JournalEntry/
JournalLine. This is the mechanism, not a rule bolted on separately, by
which "a draft never affects the financial statements."
============================================================
7. Version Evolution v0.1 -> v0.4
============================================================

+----------+---------------------------------------------+------------------------------+
| Version  | Scope                                         | Status                        |
+----------+---------------------------------------------+------------------------------+

v0.1 — Chart of Accounts / Mapping / Journal / Voucher
  Introduced the Accounting Module itself: ChartOfAccount (own GL chart),
  AccountMapping/CategoryMapping (Cash Module Account/Category -> GL
  account), LedgerMappingService.classify() (mapped/unmapped/excluded),
  JournalEntryService (journalizeEntry/processPending), VoucherService
  (1 JournalEntry = 1 Voucher). Established the "consumer, never mutator"
  boundary and the hash-routed (#/cash, #/accounting) frontend module
  switcher. Implementation PASS + Deployment Validation PASS (both
  confirmed in reports/accounting-module-v0.1-delta-report.md).

v0.2 — Financial Statements
  Added three read-only statements derived purely from v0.1 data: Trial
  Balance (beginning/period/ending balances per account), Income Statement
  (revenue/expense/net income over a range), Balance Sheet (asset/
  liability/equity as of a date, plus a presentation-only Current Earnings
  line so Assets = Liabilities + Equity + Current Earnings holds even
  though v0.1 posts no closing entries). Introduced the normal-balance sign
  convention (accountPresentation.ts) and the GL-only limitation notice
  shown on every statement. Implementation PASS + Deployment Validation
  PASS (reports/accounting-module-v0.2-delta-report.md).

v0.3 — Fiscal Period + Posting Lock
  Added FiscalPeriod (create/list/close only, no reopen) and a one-way
  posting lock: a closed period's dates can no longer receive new
  JournalEntries. Closing never posts a closing entry, never touches
  retained earnings, and never mutates any existing GL data — it is a lock
  flag only. All three statements gained an optional fiscalPeriodId
  parameter that resolves dates from a period as a single source of truth.
  Implementation PASS + Deployment Validation PASS
  (reports/accounting-module-v0.3-delta-report.md).

v0.4 — Manual Journal Entry + General Voucher  (CURRENT VERSION)
  Added GeneralVoucherDraft (a human-entered voucher scratchpad, freely
  editable while status='draft') and GeneralVoucherService.postDraft() (the
  only path that turns a draft into a real, posted JournalEntry). Added the
  formal Journal provenance contract (sourceType/sourceModule/
  sourceReferenceId) to EVERY JournalEntry, retrofitting Cash-derived
  entries onto module/CASH. Added the single shared inactive-Chart-of-
  Account enforcement point (ChartOfAccountService.getActiveOwnedAccount)
  used by BOTH posting paths. Added AccountingUnitOfWork for atomic
  (all-or-nothing) posting. Added the backward-compatible persisted-data
  migration (migrateJournalEntryProvenance) for pre-v0.4 localStorage data.
  BOTH v0.4 Implementation Gate Review AND v0.4 Prototype Deployment
  Validation are PASS (see Section 2 and the full delta report in
  Section 21) — this is the current, fully-PASSed state of the branch.
============================================================
8. Current Domain Contracts — Core Types
============================================================

(Read directly from backend/src/domain/accounting/types.ts and errors.ts —
full text in Section 21.)

ChartOfAccountType = 'asset' | 'liability' | 'equity' | 'revenue' | 'expense'
AccountingStatus    = 'active' | 'inactive'

ChartOfAccount { id, tenantId, code, name, type, status, createdAt }
  — the Accounting Module's own GL chart, independent of the Cash Module's
    Account/Category tables.

AccountMapping    { id, tenantId, cashAccountId, chartOfAccountId, createdAt }
CategoryMapping   { id, tenantId, cashCategoryName, chartOfAccountId, createdAt }
  — CategoryMapping is keyed by category NAME (a known, documented
    limitation: CashEntry.category is itself free text, not a foreign key;
    a future Cash Module category rename would silently orphan this
    mapping — acceptable for this prototype, flagged since v0.1).

MappingStatus = 'mapped' | 'unmapped' | 'excluded'
  — computed live by LedgerMappingService.classify(), never persisted, so
    it can never go stale.

JournalLine { id, journalEntryId, chartOfAccountId, debit, credit }

JournalEntry {
  id, tenantId, entryDate, amount, memo,
  sourceCashEntryId?,      // optional since v0.4; only Cash-derived entries set it
  sourceType,              // REQUIRED as of v0.4 — see Section 12
  sourceModule,            // REQUIRED as of v0.4 — see Section 12
  sourceReferenceId?,      // the id of whatever produced this entry
  createdAt,
}

*** A JournalEntry PERMANENTLY means "already posted to the GL." It is
never a draft, never provisional. There is no update or delete surface on
JournalEntry anywhere in this codebase — once created, a JournalEntry is
immutable by construction (not merely by convention). ***

Voucher { id, tenantId, voucherNo, voucherDate, journalEntryIds[], createdAt }
  — v0.1 rule, unchanged through v0.4: exactly one JournalEntry per
    Voucher, created together. journalEntryIds is an array only so a
    future, explicitly-decided version could combine multiple entries into
    one voucher without a schema change — nothing in v0.1-v0.4 does this.

FiscalPeriod { id, tenantId, name, startDate, endDate, status, closedAt?, createdAt }
  FiscalPeriodStatus = 'open' | 'closed'

GeneralVoucherDraftLine { id, chartOfAccountId, debit, credit }
GeneralVoucherDraft {
  id, tenantId, entryDate, memo, lines[], status,
  postedJournalEntryId?, postedVoucherId?, postedAt?,
  createdAt, updatedAt,
}
  GeneralVoucherDraftStatus = 'draft' | 'posted'

*** GeneralVoucherDraft is a SEPARATE ENTITY from JournalEntry. It is never
itself a JournalEntry, at any point in its lifecycle — even the moment
after it is posted, the draft row and the JournalEntry row remain two
distinct records, linked only by postedJournalEntryId. ***

AccountingErrorCode (backend/src/domain/accounting/errors.ts) includes, as
of v0.4: CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND, MAPPING_NOT_FOUND,
DUPLICATE_ACCOUNT_MAPPING, DUPLICATE_CATEGORY_MAPPING,
CASH_ENTRY_NOT_MAPPED, CASH_ENTRY_EXCLUDED, CASH_ENTRY_ALREADY_JOURNALED,
JOURNAL_ENTRY_NOT_BALANCED, FISCAL_PERIOD_INVALID_RANGE,
FISCAL_PERIOD_OVERLAPS, FISCAL_PERIOD_NOT_FOUND,
FISCAL_PERIOD_ALREADY_CLOSED, JOURNAL_ENTRY_PERIOD_CLOSED,
GENERAL_VOUCHER_DRAFT_NOT_FOUND, GENERAL_VOUCHER_ALREADY_POSTED,
GENERAL_VOUCHER_MIN_LINES, GENERAL_VOUCHER_INVALID_LINE,
GENERAL_VOUCHER_UNBALANCED, CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_INACTIVE.
============================================================
9. Persistence / Migration Model
============================================================

Backend in-memory store: AccountingDatabase (backend/src/infra/memory/
accounting/AccountingDatabase.ts) holds one Map per entity (chartOfAccounts,
accountMappings, categoryMappings, journalEntries, journalLines, vouchers,
fiscalPeriods, generalVoucherDrafts). Ids are generated by
generateAccountingId(prefix), an independent counter from the Cash Module's
own generateId — the two id spaces never need to compare or collide.

Frontend persistence (frontend/src/localdb/accounting/accountingPersistence.ts)
mirrors the Cash Module's own persistence.ts pattern: it reuses the exact
same backend AccountingDatabase/repository classes directly in the browser
(no HTTP API exists yet — see Section 17), serializing to/from a single
localStorage key, `zzcs_accounting_db_v1` (a SEPARATE key from the Cash
Module's `zzcs_prototype_db_v1` — the two databases never share storage).

loadAccountingDatabase() revives Date fields (createdAt, closedAt,
postedAt, updatedAt) from their JSON string form, then re-warms the id
counter from the max numeric suffix found across all loaded ids.

LEGACY PROVENANCE MIGRATION (v0.4): a JournalEntry persisted before v0.4
never had sourceType/sourceModule/sourceReferenceId — only
sourceCashEntryId, since v0.1-v0.3 only ever produced Cash-derived entries.
loadAccountingDatabase() runs every loaded JournalEntry row through
migrateJournalEntryProvenance() (frontend/src/localdb/accounting/
journalProvenanceMigration.ts) BEFORE any service code ever touches the
data:
  - already has sourceType AND sourceModule -> returned as-is (a new
    object, same values; the function never returns the same reference)
  - has sourceCashEntryId, missing provenance -> backfilled to
    sourceType='module', sourceModule='CASH', sourceReferenceId=
    sourceCashEntryId (sourceCashEntryId itself is left unchanged)
  - neither -> an unrecognized/corrupt shape. THROWS rather than silently
    guessing a default or producing a partially-loaded database. Nothing
    is written back to localStorage before the throw propagates, so a
    failed load can never corrupt or lose the original persisted content
    (verified directly by a test that asserts localStorage's raw content
    is byte-for-byte unchanged after a load that throws).

This migration function is pure (never mutates its input, always returns a
new object) and deliberately kept in its own file with no localStorage/DOM
dependency, specifically so it can be imported and unit-tested from the
backend's vitest suite (backend/test/accounting/
journalProvenanceMigration.test.ts) even though it is frontend code — the
frontend itself gained its own vitest+jsdom setup later in v0.4 (see Section
15) to also test the real persistence boundary
(accountingPersistence.test.ts), not just the pure function in isolation.
============================================================
10. Fiscal Period / Posting Lock
============================================================

FiscalPeriodService (backend/src/domain/accounting/services/
FiscalPeriodService.ts) supports CREATE, LIST, and CLOSE only — there is no
reopen operation anywhere in the codebase.

- createFiscalPeriod: validates startDate <= endDate
  (FISCAL_PERIOD_INVALID_RANGE) and rejects any date-range overlap with an
  existing period of the SAME tenant (FISCAL_PERIOD_OVERLAPS). Gaps
  (dates covered by no period) are allowed. An identical range for a
  DIFFERENT tenant is unrelated and allowed (tenant isolation).
- closePeriod: one-directional. Sets status='closed' and records closedAt.
  Closing an already-closed period throws FISCAL_PERIOD_ALREADY_CLOSED
  rather than silently no-op-ing.
- isDateInClosedPeriod(tenantId, date): inclusive boundary check
  (startDate <= date <= endDate). A date outside every defined period is
  never considered closed.

*** Closing a Fiscal Period is a POSTING LOCK ONLY. It is explicitly NOT a
full accounting close: closing NEVER posts a closing JournalEntry, NEVER
touches retained earnings, and NEVER mutates any existing GL data. It only
blocks NEW journalization (both the Cash-mapping path and the v0.4 manual
voucher path) for dates that fall inside the closed range. Because
JournalEntry has no update/delete surface anywhere in this codebase, "no
modification of history" inside a closed period was already true by
construction before v0.3 added the lock — v0.3/v0.4 only add the lock on
NEW postings. ***

Both posting paths check the lock at the same semantic point:
  - JournalEntryService.journalizeEntry / processPending (Cash path)
  - GeneralVoucherService.postDraft (Manual path, v0.4)
both call fiscalPeriodService.isDateInClosedPeriod(tenantId, entryDate) and
reject with JOURNAL_ENTRY_PERIOD_CLOSED, creating zero artifacts, if the
date falls inside a closed period.

All three financial statements accept an optional fiscalPeriodId that
resolves fromDate/toDate/asOfDate from the period as a single source of
truth (Trial Balance/Income Statement: fromDate=period.startDate,
toDate/asOfDate=period.endDate; Balance Sheet: asOfDate=period.endDate
ONLY — period.startDate is never read by BalanceSheetService, since a
Balance Sheet is inherently an as-of statement).
============================================================
11. Manual Journal Entry / General Voucher
============================================================

GeneralVoucherService (backend/src/domain/accounting/services/
GeneralVoucherService.ts) is the v0.4 addition that lets a human post
directly to the GL without going through a CashEntry.

Lifecycle:
  createDraft / updateDraft / deleteDraft / listDrafts / getDraft — freely
  usable while status='draft'. NO strict validation happens at create or
  update time: lines may be incomplete, unbalanced, reference nothing, or
  number fewer than 2 — a draft is a scratchpad, not a commitment. Deleting
  or updating an already-posted draft throws GENERAL_VOUCHER_ALREADY_POSTED.

  postDraft(tenantId, draftId) is the ONLY path in the entire codebase that
  ever turns a draft into a real JournalEntry. It runs entirely inside
  AccountingUnitOfWork.runAtomic() (see Section 8/9's atomicity note and
  Section 13's cross-reference) and validates, in this fixed order:
    1. draft exists (tenant-scoped) and is not already posted
    2. at least 2 lines                          -> GENERAL_VOUCHER_MIN_LINES
    3. every line individually well-formed (a chartOfAccountId is present;
       debit/credit are not negative; not both debit>0 and credit>0; not
       both zero)                                -> GENERAL_VOUCHER_INVALID_LINE
    4. every line's ChartOfAccount exists, is owned by this tenant, and is
       ACTIVE (the single shared enforcement point — see Section 12)
                              -> CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND / CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_INACTIVE
    5. SigmaDebit === SigmaCredit within a small epsilon (1e-9)
                                                  -> GENERAL_VOUCHER_UNBALANCED
    6. entryDate is not inside a closed FiscalPeriod
                                                  -> JOURNAL_ENTRY_PERIOD_CLOSED

  ANY failure at any of these steps creates ZERO artifacts (proven directly
  by tests at every failure point, including a late-injected failure after
  most mutations have already run — see Section 15).

  On success, EXACTLY ONE JournalEntry is created (sourceType='manual',
  sourceModule='GL', sourceReferenceId=draft.id, sourceCashEntryId left
  undefined), with N JournalLines (matching the draft's lines 1:1), plus
  EXACTLY ONE Voucher (via the same VoucherService.createForJournalEntry
  the Cash path uses). The draft itself is then updated, in the same atomic
  transaction, to: status='posted', postedJournalEntryId=<the new
  JournalEntry's id>, postedVoucherId=<the new Voucher's id>,
  postedAt=<now>. All three posted-metadata fields are always set together
  — never independently of each other.

  A posted draft can never be updated, deleted, or re-posted
  (GENERAL_VOUCHER_ALREADY_POSTED on any of these). A posted manual
  JournalEntry likewise cannot be directly modified or deleted in v0.4 —
  this is deferred, by design, to a future reversal/adjustment workflow
  (see Section 19; NOT started, NOT authorized by this document).
============================================================
12. Provenance Contract
============================================================

Every JournalEntry, regardless of producer, carries:

  sourceType   : 'manual' | 'module' | 'system'
  sourceModule : 'GL' | 'CASH' | 'AP' | 'AR' | 'FA' | 'PAYROLL' | 'INVENTORY' | 'SYSTEM'
  sourceReferenceId? : string   // id of whatever produced this entry, scoped
                                 // within sourceModule

As of v0.4, only two (sourceType, sourceModule) combinations are ever
actually produced by any code in this repository:

  Cash Module mapping (JournalEntryService.journalizeEntry/processPending):
    sourceType='module', sourceModule='CASH', sourceReferenceId=<CashEntry id>,
    sourceCashEntryId=<the same CashEntry id> (kept, for backward
    compatibility with pre-v0.4 code/data that only ever used
    sourceCashEntryId).

  Manual General Voucher (GeneralVoucherService.postDraft):
    sourceType='manual', sourceModule='GL', sourceReferenceId=<GeneralVoucherDraft id>,
    sourceCashEntryId is left undefined.

'system'/'SYSTEM' and the AP/AR/FA/PAYROLL/INVENTORY sourceModule values are
declared now, in the type union only, as future extension points — NO code
anywhere in v0.1-v0.4 ever produces an entry with any of these values. This
is intentional: it means the provenance model will not need another
breaking type change when those future modules eventually post their own
journal entries; they will simply pick the matching sourceModule and follow
the same contract already established here.

sourceCashEntryId itself became OPTIONAL on JournalEntry as of v0.4 (it was
required in v0.1-v0.3); it is now a legacy/convenience field retained
purely for backward Cash-Module traceability, superseded in general by
sourceType/sourceModule/sourceReferenceId as the authoritative provenance
model. Manual/GL entries never set it.
============================================================
13. Financial Statements
============================================================

Three read-only reports, all derived purely from JournalEntry/JournalLine/
ChartOfAccount data (never from GeneralVoucherDraft, never from Cash Module
data directly):

  TrialBalanceService.getTrialBalance(tenantId, { fromDate?, asOfDate?, fiscalPeriodId? })
    Per ChartOfAccount: beginningBalance (net activity strictly before
    fromDate, 0 if fromDate omitted), periodDebit/periodCredit (raw, never
    sign-flipped, activity within [fromDate, asOfDate]), endingBalance
    (beginning + period movement, normalized). SigmaperiodDebit ===
    SigmaperiodCredit always, by construction, since every JournalEntry the
    system creates is itself balanced.

  IncomeStatementService.getIncomeStatement(tenantId, { fromDate?, toDate?, fiscalPeriodId? })
    Revenue/expense lines over the range; netIncome = totalRevenue -
    totalExpense.

  BalanceSheetService.getBalanceSheet(tenantId, asOfDate | { asOfDate?, fiscalPeriodId? })
    Asset/liability/equity lines as of a date, PLUS a presentation-only
    currentEarnings line (computed by calling IncomeStatementService for
    the same asOfDate — never a separate calculation, so it can never
    drift), so that
      totalAssets === totalLiabilities + totalEquity + currentEarnings
    holds even though NO closing JournalEntry is ever posted anywhere in
    this codebase (there is no closing-entries feature at all as of v0.4 —
    see Section 17/19).

Normal-balance sign convention (accountPresentation.ts, shared by all
three): asset/expense are debit-normal; liability/equity/revenue are
credit-normal. normalize() flips sign only for credit-normal types and
NEVER clamps an abnormal balance to zero or forces it positive — a
liability with an unusual debit balance stays visible as a negative number,
by explicit design and covered directly by tests.

Every statement carries and displays the exact GL-only limitation notice
(GL_ONLY_LIMITATION_NOTICE, defined once in types.ts):

  "本報表僅反映已進入總帳（journalized）的交易；尚未設定科目對應、未處理
  轉帳，以及記帳模組帳戶的期初餘額均不包含在內，因此不代表 Cash Module
  全部帳務餘額。"

*** A GeneralVoucherDraft with status='draft' NEVER affects any of these
three statements. This is not a special-cased filter — the statements
never read the draft table at all, only JournalEntry/JournalLine, and a
draft is not a JournalEntry until GeneralVoucherService.postDraft()
succeeds. This is verified directly by tests that query all three
statements before and after posting the same draft. ***
============================================================
14. UI / Routing / Module Structure
============================================================

Routing: a minimal hand-rolled hash router (no React Router dependency),
giving each module a real, directly-openable, bookmarkable, shareable URL:
  #/cash          -> the existing Cash Module UI (unchanged)
  #/accounting    -> AccountingPage.tsx (this module)
The root URL with no hash still defaults to the Cash Module.

AccountingPage.tsx (frontend/src/features/accounting/AccountingPage.tsx)
wraps its children in AccountingDataProvider and renders one top nav plus
these sections in order, each an independent component:
  #sec-accounting-dashboard  AccountingDashboard.tsx  — pending/unmapped/
                                                          excluded/journaled
                                                          counts, the
                                                          "產生分錄"
                                                          (processPending)
                                                          action button
  #sec-coa                   ChartOfAccountsManage.tsx — GL chart CRUD,
                                                          Account/Category
                                                          mapping tables,
                                                          per-COA
                                                          active/inactive
                                                          toggle, stale-
                                                          mapping warning
                                                          badges
  #sec-fiscal-periods         FiscalPeriodsView.tsx     — period create/list/
                                                          close
  #sec-general-voucher         GeneralVoucherView.tsx    — manual voucher
                                                          draft create form
                                                          (dynamic add/
                                                          remove line rows),
                                                          draft list with
                                                          Post/Delete
  #sec-journal-entries         JournalEntryList.tsx      — posted entries,
                                                          incl. "來源"
                                                          (source) column
  #sec-vouchers                 VoucherView.tsx           — posted vouchers
  #sec-trial-balance            TrialBalanceView.tsx
  #sec-income-statement         IncomeStatementView.tsx
  #sec-balance-sheet            BalanceSheetView.tsx

Local persistence: frontend/src/localdb/accounting/AccountingDataProvider.tsx
initializes the whole Accounting Module service graph once per app lifetime
via initAccountingServices() (accountingServices.ts), which itself calls
loadAccountingDatabase() (accountingPersistence.ts) to read from the
`zzcs_accounting_db_v1` localStorage key, and seeds a demo Chart of Accounts
+ mappings on first load if none exists yet (seedDemoChartOfAccounts). Every
mutating UI action calls `accounting.save()` immediately afterward to
persist the change back to localStorage — there is no separate autosave
timer or debounce.

      loadAccountingDatabase() throw, and leaves localStorage's raw content
      byte-for-byte untouched

                                  Module regression

                                  income->journal->voucher, unmapped

                                  excluded-but-counted, reload persistence

                                  Balance Sheet correctness via the real UI
    fiscalPeriod.spec.ts        (v0.3) — period create/close, posting lock
                                  blocks a new entry without aborting other
                                  pending entries, fiscal-period-selector
                                  statement queries
    generalVoucher.spec.ts      (v0.4) — draft creation excluded from Trial
                                  Balance pre-post, included post-post,
                                  correct 來源 label, unbalanced draft
                                  rejected with a visible error, posting
                                  blocked after deactivating a target COA,
                                  Cash Module/v0.1-v0.3 flows unaffected

  All 5 specs are confirmed, per reports/accounting-module-v0.4-delta-
  report.md, to have passed both locally (dev server) and against the real
  deployed GitHub Pages preview (see Section 16), with one documented,
  investigated, non-blocking flaky failure of financialStatements.spec.ts
  in the deployed-preview batched run (sandbox network egress contention,
  not a code regression — isolated rerun passed clean; see the full report
  text in Section 21 for the complete investigation).

16. GitHub Pages Preview / Deployment Workflow
============================================================

.github/workflows/deploy-pages-preview.yml (full text in Section 21):

  Trigger: push to `feature/accounting-module` touching `frontend/**` or
  the workflow file itself, plus manual workflow_dispatch.

  What it builds: this repo's single GitHub Pages site is made to serve
  TWO builds from one combined artifact, since GitHub Pages can only serve
  one live deployment per site:
    /                                  -> production root, built from
                                           `main` UNCHANGED (still just the
                                           frozen Cash Service v1.0)
    /preview/accounting-module/        -> this feature branch's build,
                                           with PAGES_BASE_PATH set to
                                           /zanzan-cash-service/preview/accounting-module/

  This avoids a second GitHub Pages site, a new external hosting
  credential, or touching the already-reviewed production deployment.

  Live preview URL (per the v0.4 deployment-validation delta report,
  confirmed HTTP 200 at that time):
    https://b88104069.github.io/zanzan-cash-service/preview/accounting-module/

  playwright.preview.config.ts runs the same e2e specs (frontend/e2e/**)
  against that live preview URL instead of localhost, with sandbox-proxy
  workarounds (HTTPS_PROXY, --disable-http2) mirroring
  playwright.prod.config.ts's existing approach for the production root.

  Known non-blocking cosmetic item, carried unresolved since v0.2's
  deployment-validation Gate and still true at v0.4: the workflow's `name:`
  field still literally reads "Deploy Accounting Module v0.1 preview to
  GitHub Pages" even though it now builds v0.1 through v0.4. This is a
  display-name-only issue (see the full text in Section 21) — the
  workflow's actual behavior (trigger, build, combine, deploy) is correct
  and unaffected.
============================================================
17. Known Limitations
============================================================

Read directly from reports/accounting-module-v0.4-delta-report.md's own
"RISKS / KNOWN LIMITATIONS" section (full text in Section 21) and from the
v0.1-v0.3 architecture docs, not invented for this handoff:

  - A posted manual journal entry (General Voucher) cannot be modified or
    deleted in v0.4. This is intentional, deferred to a future v0.6
    reversal/adjustment workflow — not an oversight or a bug.
  - GeneralVoucherDraft validation is deliberately lenient at create/update
    time (incomplete/unbalanced/invalid lines allowed) and strict only at
    postDraft — by design, so a draft functions as genuine scratch space.
  - AccountingUnitOfWork's snapshot/restore atomicity mechanism is specific
    to this in-memory prototype; a real database would replace it with an
    actual transaction. The `runAtomic()` interface is shaped so that swap
    requires no change to GeneralVoucherService, but the swap itself has
    NOT been done — there is no real database anywhere in this module.
  - No closing entries exist anywhere in the codebase. Balance Sheet's
    Current Earnings line is presentation-only, computed live every time,
    never posted as a JournalEntry. There is no year-end/period-end close
    process beyond FiscalPeriod's posting lock (Section 10).
  - No reopen of a closed FiscalPeriod exists anywhere in the codebase.
  - Unmapped Cash entries, excluded transfers, and Cash Module account
    opening balances are NOT reflected in any financial statement — this
    is disclosed verbatim on every statement via GL_ONLY_LIMITATION_NOTICE
    (Section 13), carried over unchanged since v0.2.
  - CategoryMapping is keyed by category name, not a stable id — a future
    Cash Module category rename would silently orphan the mapping (known
    limitation since v0.1, unchanged through v0.4).
  - No tax logic, no multi-currency, no consolidated/multi-entity
    statements — out of scope for the entire Accounting Module effort as
    currently scoped, not just deferred within v0.4.
  - No HTTP/REST API exists for the Accounting Module. The frontend calls
    the backend domain/service classes directly in-process (via
    accountingServices.ts), the same pattern the Cash Module's own
    frontend uses for its local-first prototype. There is no server
    deployment of the Accounting Module's backend code as of this commit.
  - Single-tenant-per-browser-session in practice: the Accounting Module's
    services are fully tenant-scoped in their APIs and tested for tenant
    isolation, but the frontend wiring only ever operates against
    `cash.tenantId`, the single tenant the Cash Module's own local session
    is bound to — there is no UI for switching or managing multiple
    tenants from the Accounting Module.
============================================================
18. Explicitly Out of Scope / Not Yet Started
============================================================

Per the parent project's kickoff contract (see /CLAUDE.md and
docs/gates/kickoff-contract.md at the repo root) and the Accounting
Module's own architecture docs:

  - No production cutover of any kind, for the Cash Module OR the
    Accounting Module. The project ends at a staging/non-production
    deployment; the live WordPress site, WooCommerce, and real
    customer/member data are never touched by anything in this repository.
  - No WooCommerce production integration anywhere in this module.
  - No OCR, no AI bookkeeping, no mobile app.
  - No auth/login system for the Accounting Module specifically (it rides
    on whatever session/tenant context the Cash Module's local prototype
    already establishes).
  - No multi-user concurrent-editing support.
  - No merge of `feature/accounting-module` into `main` has been done, and
    none is authorized by this document (standing Option B decision — see
    Section 2).
  - No v0.5 work (Closing Entries + Retained Earnings) or v0.6 work
    (Reopen/Reversal/Adjustment Workflow) has been started. Both are
    reviewer-suggested FUTURE candidates only — see Section 19. This
    handoff document is a description of v0.1-v0.4's completed, PASSed
    state; it is not a plan and not an authorization to begin v0.5/v0.6.
============================================================
19. Important Future Architecture Notes
============================================================

*** EVERYTHING IN THIS SECTION IS NOT STARTED / FUTURE CANDIDATE ONLY. ***
*** Nothing below describes current v0.4 behavior, and nothing below is ***
*** authorized to begin by this handoff document. It is recorded here  ***
*** purely as guidance for whoever scopes a future version, exactly as ***
*** it appears in the v0.4 Gate Review's own non-blocking note.        ***

v0.5 candidate — Closing Entries + Retained Earnings (NOT STARTED)
  The v0.4 Gate Review reviewer's own non-blocking suggestion (quoted
  directly from reports/accounting-module-v0.4-delta-report.md's Gate
  Review Result, Section 21 has the full text): if a future v0.5 adds
  closing entries and retained-earnings transfer, it should REUSE this
  round's provenance/atomic-posting foundation — i.e. post closing entries
  through the SAME AccountingUnitOfWork/GL path, using
  sourceType='system' and sourceModule='SYSTEM' (both already declared as
  extension points in the type union, per Section 12, but never produced
  by any code as of v0.4) — RATHER THAN building a second, parallel ledger
  path for the closing process. This is explicitly out of scope for v0.4
  and is guidance only, not a v0.4 unfinished item.

v0.6 candidate — Reopen / Reversal / Adjustment Workflow (NOT STARTED)
  v0.4's own architecture doc and delta report both explicitly defer "a
  posted manual journal entry cannot be modified or deleted" to a future
  reversal/adjustment workflow, tentatively numbered v0.6 in the delta
  report's own text. No design for this workflow exists anywhere in the
  repository as of this commit — no types, no service stubs, no tests, no
  UI. This is a name for a future scope only, not a partially-built
  feature.

These are recorded here strictly as forward-looking guidance the v0.4 Gate
Review reviewer left for "whoever scopes v0.5" — they do not constitute a
committed roadmap, a task list, or permission to start either one.

============================================================
FILE: backend/src/domain/accounting/types.ts
============================================================

// Accounting Module Prototype v0.1 domain types.
//
// This module is a CONSUMER of the Cash Module (backend/src/domain/types.ts,
// backend/src/infra/memory/InMemoryDatabase.ts) — it reads CashEntry/Account
// records but never writes to them, and keeps its own data in a fully
// separate store (see backend/src/infra/memory/accounting/AccountingDatabase.ts).
// See docs/architecture/accounting-module-v0.1.md for the full design and
// the Slack Gate Review thread that approved this plan before any code was
// written.

export type ChartOfAccountType = 'asset' | 'liability' | 'equity' | 'revenue' | 'expense';
export type AccountingStatus = 'active' | 'inactive';

/** The Accounting Module's own general ledger chart — independent of the Cash Module's Account/Category tables. */
export interface ChartOfAccount {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  code: string;
  name: string;
  type: ChartOfAccountType;
  status: AccountingStatus;
  createdAt: Date;
}

/**
 * Maps one Cash Module Account (by id) to one GL account. Reload-safe: keyed
 * by the Cash Module's own stable account id, not by name.
 */
export interface AccountMapping {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  cashAccountId: string;
  chartOfAccountId: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

/**
 * Maps one Cash Module Category (by its free-text name — CashEntry.category
 * is itself a free-text string, not a foreign key; see
 * docs/architecture/category-decision.md) to one GL account.
 *
 * Known limitation (flagged by Gate Review, recorded in the v0.1 Delta
 * Report): keying by name means a future category rename in the Cash
 * Module would silently orphan this mapping. Acceptable for a
 * single-tenant prototype; a production version should key by a stable
 * category identifier once the Cash Module category model supports one.
 */
export interface CategoryMapping {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  cashCategoryName: string;
  chartOfAccountId: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

export type MappingStatus = 'mapped' | 'unmapped' | 'excluded';

/** One classified CashEntry, before or after journaling. Computed live from CashEntry + mapping tables — never persisted as its own row, so it can never go stale. */
export interface MappingClassification {
  cashEntryId: string;
  status: MappingStatus;
  /** Present only when status === 'mapped'. */
  debitChartOfAccountId?: string;
  creditChartOfAccountId?: string;
  /** Human-readable reason, e.g. which side of the mapping is missing, or "transfer". */
  reason?: string;
}

/** One line of a journal entry. v0.1 entries always have exactly one debit line and one credit line, but the model itself is not limited to two lines. */
export interface JournalLine {
  id: string;
  journalEntryId: string;
  chartOfAccountId: string;
  debit: number;
  credit: number;
}

export interface JournalEntry {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  entryDate: string; // ISO date, copied from the source CashEntry at journaling time (or from the GeneralVoucherDraft, v0.4)
  amount: number;
  memo: string;
  /**
   * Traceability back to the Cash Module — reference only, never written
   * back to. Optional as of v0.4: only Cash-derived JournalEntries set
   * this; manual-voucher-derived entries never do. Kept for backward
   * compatibility with pre-v0.4 persisted data — see
   * `sourceType`/`sourceModule`/`sourceReferenceId` below for the general
   * provenance model that superseded it.
   */
  sourceCashEntryId?: string;
  /** See "Journal provenance model" (v0.4) below — required for every JournalEntry created from v0.4 onward. */
  sourceType: JournalEntrySourceType;
  sourceModule: JournalEntrySourceModule;
  /** The id of whatever produced this entry within sourceModule — a CashEntry id for CASH, a GeneralVoucherDraft id for manual/GL. */
  sourceReferenceId?: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

export interface Voucher {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  voucherNo: string; // e.g. "JV00001"
  voucherDate: string; // ISO date
  /** v0.1: always exactly one id (1 JournalEntry = 1 Voucher). Kept as an array so a later, explicitly-decided version can support combining multiple entries into one voucher without a data model change. */
  journalEntryIds: string[];
  createdAt: Date;
}

// --- Accounting Module v0.2 — Financial Statements ---
//
// Trial Balance / Income Statement / Balance Sheet are all read-only
// derivations over JournalEntry/JournalLine/ChartOfAccount — see
// backend/src/domain/accounting/services/{TrialBalance,IncomeStatement,
// BalanceSheet}Service.ts and docs/architecture/accounting-module-v0.2.md.
// Approved by Gate Review (Slack #ai-gate-test) after a revision that
// fixed two real accounting-correctness gaps in the first draft plan:
// Trial Balance needed beginning/period/ending balances (not a bare
// date-range activity sum), and Balance Sheet needed a presentation-only
// Current Earnings line (v0.1 has no closing entries, so without this
// Assets would not equal Liabilities + Equity).

/** Every ChartOfAccount type has a "normal" side. A positive normalized balance means the account is in its normal direction; negative means reversed — never clamped away. */
export type NormalBalanceSide = 'debit' | 'credit';

/**
 * The single, exact wording Gate Review required for every financial
 * statement view — names the three concrete gaps (unmapped, excluded
 * transfers, Cash Module opening balances) rather than a vague
 * "GL-reflected only" disclaimer.
 */
export const GL_ONLY_LIMITATION_NOTICE =
  '本報表僅反映已進入總帳（journalized）的交易；尚未設定科目對應、未處理轉帳，以及記帳模組帳戶的期初餘額均不包含在內，因此不代表 Cash Module 全部帳務餘額。';

export interface TrialBalanceLine {
  chartOfAccountId: string;
  code: string;
  name: string;
  type: ChartOfAccountType;
  normalBalance: NormalBalanceSide;
  /** Normalized (positive = normal direction) balance immediately before fromDate; 0 when fromDate is omitted. */
  beginningBalance: number;
  /** Raw Σdebit / Σcredit within [fromDate, asOfDate] — never sign-flipped, always >= 0. */
  periodDebit: number;
  periodCredit: number;
  /** Normalized ending balance = beginningBalance's raw value +/- period movement, then normalized. */
  endingBalance: number;
}

export interface TrialBalanceReport {
  tenantId: string;
  fromDate?: string;
  asOfDate: string;
  /** Echoed only when the report was resolved from a FiscalPeriod (v0.3); undefined for an explicit-date query. */
  fiscalPeriodId?: string;
  lines: TrialBalanceLine[];
  totalPeriodDebit: number;
  totalPeriodCredit: number;
  limitationNotice: string;
}

export interface IncomeStatementLine {
  chartOfAccountId: string;
  code: string;
  name: string;
  type: 'revenue' | 'expense';
  /** Normalized (credit-normal for revenue, debit-normal for expense) amount over the period. */
  amount: number;
}

export interface IncomeStatementReport {
  tenantId: string;
  fromDate?: string;
  toDate: string;
  /** Echoed only when the report was resolved from a FiscalPeriod (v0.3); undefined for an explicit-date query. */
  fiscalPeriodId?: string;
  revenueLines: IncomeStatementLine[];
  expenseLines: IncomeStatementLine[];
  totalRevenue: number;
  totalExpense: number;
  netIncome: number;
  limitationNotice: string;
}

export interface BalanceSheetLine {
  chartOfAccountId: string;
  code: string;
  name: string;
  type: 'asset' | 'liability' | 'equity';
  normalBalance: NormalBalanceSide;
  /** Normalized (positive = normal direction) balance as of asOfDate. */
  balance: number;
}

export interface BalanceSheetReport {
  tenantId: string;
  asOfDate: string;
  /** Echoed only when the report was resolved from a FiscalPeriod (v0.3); undefined for an explicit-date query. */
  fiscalPeriodId?: string;
  assetLines: BalanceSheetLine[];
  liabilityLines: BalanceSheetLine[];
  equityLines: BalanceSheetLine[];
  totalAssets: number;
  totalLiabilities: number;
  totalEquity: number;
  /** Presentation-only: cumulativeRevenue - cumulativeExpense as of asOfDate, computed by IncomeStatementService — never a separate calculation. No closing JournalEntry is ever posted for this. */
  currentEarnings: number;
  /** totalLiabilities + totalEquity + currentEarnings — should equal totalAssets within GL-reflected scope. */
  totalLiabilitiesAndEquity: number;
  limitationNotice: string;
}

// --- Accounting Module v0.3 — Fiscal Period + Close Lock ---
//
// A Fiscal Period is a named, non-overlapping (per tenant) date range that
// can be closed to lock further journalization within it. v0.3 is
// deliberately narrow — see docs/architecture/accounting-module-v0.3.md and
// the Slack Gate Review thread that approved this plan:
//   - create / list / close only; NO reopen (closing is one-directional).
//   - closing sets a lock flag only; it NEVER posts a closing JournalEntry,
//     never touches retained earnings, never mutates GL data.
//   - the lock only blocks NEW journalization (JournalEntryService has no
//     update/delete surface in this codebase, so "no modify" is already
//     true by construction — not something this Gate needs to add).
//   - financial statements may optionally resolve their dates from a
//     FiscalPeriod (fiscalPeriodId) instead of explicit dates, but the
//     resolution is a single source of truth (see TrialBalance/
//     IncomeStatement/BalanceSheetService) — never a second calculation.

// --- Accounting Module v0.4 — Manual Journal Entry + General Voucher ---
//
// See docs/architecture/accounting-module-v0.4.md for the full design and
// the Gate Review thread that approved this plan (3 rounds, final PASS)
// before any code was written.
//
// Core rule: `JournalEntry` permanently means "already posted to GL". A
// draft manual voucher is a SEPARATE entity, `GeneralVoucherDraft`, never
// itself a `JournalEntry` — it never affects Trial Balance / Income
// Statement / Balance Sheet while unposted. Posting a draft writes into the
// SAME JournalEntry/JournalLine/Voucher tables Cash-derived entries use —
// there is only ever one GL, never a second ledger for manual postings.

/** Every JournalEntry now carries where it came from. 'manual' = a human posted a General Voucher directly; 'module' = produced by another module's own posting logic; 'system' = reserved for future system-generated entries (e.g. closing entries) — not produced anywhere in v0.4. */
export type JournalEntrySourceType = 'manual' | 'module' | 'system';

/** Which module produced the entry. Only 'GL' (manual voucher) and 'CASH' (Cash Module mapping) are actually produced in v0.4 — AP/AR/FA/PAYROLL/INVENTORY/SYSTEM are declared now as future extension points so the provenance model doesn't need another breaking change when those modules arrive. */
export type JournalEntrySourceModule = 'GL' | 'CASH' | 'AP' | 'AR' | 'FA' | 'PAYROLL' | 'INVENTORY' | 'SYSTEM';

export type GeneralVoucherDraftStatus = 'draft' | 'posted';

/** One line of a General Voucher draft. Freely addable/removable/editable while the draft is 'draft'; frozen once posted (the resulting JournalLine is the permanent record from then on). */
export interface GeneralVoucherDraftLine {
  id: string;
  chartOfAccountId: string;
  debit: number; // >= 0
  credit: number; // >= 0
}

/**
 * A manually-entered journal voucher before it is posted to the GL. Lines
 * may be incomplete or unbalanced while status === 'draft' — full
 * validation happens only at `GeneralVoucherService.postDraft` time. Once
 * posted, a draft is immutable (no update/delete) and permanently linked to
 * the JournalEntry it produced via `postedJournalEntryId`.
 */
export interface GeneralVoucherDraft {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  entryDate: string; // ISO date
  memo: string;
  lines: GeneralVoucherDraftLine[];
  status: GeneralVoucherDraftStatus;
  postedJournalEntryId?: string;
  /** Set together with postedJournalEntryId/postedAt at post time — the id of the Voucher created for postedJournalEntryId. Never set independently of the other two. */
  postedVoucherId?: string;
  /** Set together with postedJournalEntryId/postedVoucherId at post time — when this draft was posted. Never set independently of the other two. */
  postedAt?: Date;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

export type FiscalPeriodStatus = 'open' | 'closed';

/** A tenant-scoped accounting period. Periods must not overlap another period of the same tenant; gaps (dates covered by no period) are allowed and unaffected by any close. */
export interface FiscalPeriod {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  startDate: string; // ISO date, inclusive
  endDate: string; // ISO date, inclusive; startDate <= endDate
  status: FiscalPeriodStatus;
  closedAt?: Date;
  createdAt: Date;
}

FILE: backend/src/domain/accounting/errors.ts

// Accounting Module's own error type — deliberately NOT sharing Cash
// Module's DomainError/DomainErrorCode (backend/src/domain/errors.ts),
// which is frozen v1.0 baseline. Keeping error types separate mirrors the
// rest of the module's isolation (own types, own storage, own services).

export type AccountingErrorCode =
  | 'CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND'
  | 'MAPPING_NOT_FOUND'
  | 'DUPLICATE_ACCOUNT_MAPPING'
  | 'DUPLICATE_CATEGORY_MAPPING'
  | 'CASH_ENTRY_NOT_MAPPED'
  | 'CASH_ENTRY_EXCLUDED'
  | 'CASH_ENTRY_ALREADY_JOURNALED'
  | 'JOURNAL_ENTRY_NOT_BALANCED'
  | 'FISCAL_PERIOD_INVALID_RANGE'
  | 'FISCAL_PERIOD_OVERLAPS'
  | 'FISCAL_PERIOD_NOT_FOUND'
  | 'FISCAL_PERIOD_ALREADY_CLOSED'
  | 'JOURNAL_ENTRY_PERIOD_CLOSED'
  | 'GENERAL_VOUCHER_DRAFT_NOT_FOUND'
  | 'GENERAL_VOUCHER_ALREADY_POSTED'
  | 'GENERAL_VOUCHER_MIN_LINES'
  | 'GENERAL_VOUCHER_INVALID_LINE'
  | 'GENERAL_VOUCHER_UNBALANCED'
  | 'CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_INACTIVE';

export class AccountingError extends Error {
  readonly code: AccountingErrorCode;

  constructor(code: AccountingErrorCode, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'AccountingError';
    this.code = code;
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend/src/domain/accounting/services/BalanceSheetService.ts
============================================================

import type { ChartOfAccountService } from './ChartOfAccountService.js';
import type { FiscalPeriodService } from './FiscalPeriodService.js';
import type { IncomeStatementService } from './IncomeStatementService.js';
import type { JournalEntryService } from './JournalEntryService.js';
import { normalBalanceOf, normalize } from './accountPresentation.js';
import { GL_ONLY_LIMITATION_NOTICE } from '../types.js';
import type { BalanceSheetLine, BalanceSheetReport } from '../types.js';

export interface BalanceSheetInput {
  asOfDate?: string;
  /**
   * v0.3: resolve asOfDate from a FiscalPeriod — as-of = period.endDate
   * ONLY. Balance Sheet is an as-of statement, not a range statement, so
   * period.startDate is never used here. When given, asOfDate above is
   * ignored. currentEarnings below still accumulates since inception
   * through that endDate — fiscalPeriodId never narrows Current Earnings
   * to just that period's activity (that would break the v0.2 balance
   * invariant, since v0.1 posts no closing entries).
   */
  fiscalPeriodId?: string;
}

/**
 * Balance Sheet (資產負債表) — asset/liability/equity ChartOfAccounts as of
 * a date, plus a presentation-only Current Earnings line (Gate Review MUST
 * FIX: without it, Assets would not equal Liabilities + Equity, since v0.1
 * posts no closing entries and revenue/expense balances never move into
 * equity). currentEarnings is always computed via IncomeStatementService
 * for the same asOfDate — never a separate calculation, so it can never
 * drift from what the Income Statement itself reports.
 */
export class BalanceSheetService {
  constructor(
    private readonly journalEntryService: JournalEntryService,
    private readonly chartOfAccountService: ChartOfAccountService,
    private readonly incomeStatementService: IncomeStatementService,
    private readonly fiscalPeriodService?: FiscalPeriodService,
  ) {}

  /** `asOfDateOrInput` accepts a plain ISO date (v0.2 explicit-date call, unchanged) or a v0.3 input object with fiscalPeriodId. */
  async getBalanceSheet(tenantId: string, asOfDateOrInput: string | BalanceSheetInput): Promise<BalanceSheetReport> {
    const input: BalanceSheetInput = typeof asOfDateOrInput === 'string' ? { asOfDate: asOfDateOrInput } : asOfDateOrInput;

    let asOfDate = input.asOfDate;
    if (input.fiscalPeriodId !== undefined) {
      const period = await this.fiscalPeriodService!.getFiscalPeriod(tenantId, input.fiscalPeriodId);
      asOfDate = period.endDate;
    }
    if (asOfDate === undefined) {
      throw new Error('BalanceSheetService.getBalanceSheet requires either asOfDate or fiscalPeriodId');
    }

    const [accounts, entries, incomeStatement] = await Promise.all([
      this.chartOfAccountService.listChartOfAccounts(tenantId),
      this.journalEntryService.listJournalEntries(tenantId),
      this.incomeStatementService.getIncomeStatement(tenantId, { toDate: asOfDate }),
    ]);

    const relevantEntries = entries.filter((entry) => entry.entryDate <= asOfDate);

    function balanceFor(chartOfAccountId: string): number {
      let raw = 0;
      for (const entry of relevantEntries) {
        for (const line of entry.lines) {
          if (line.chartOfAccountId === chartOfAccountId) raw += line.debit - line.credit;
        }
      }
      return raw;
    }

    const toLine = (account: (typeof accounts)[number]): BalanceSheetLine => ({
      chartOfAccountId: account.id,
      code: account.code,
      name: account.name,
      type: account.type as 'asset' | 'liability' | 'equity',
      normalBalance: normalBalanceOf(account.type),
      balance: normalize(balanceFor(account.id), account.type),
    });

    const assetLines = accounts.filter((a) => a.type === 'asset').map(toLine);
    const liabilityLines = accounts.filter((a) => a.type === 'liability').map(toLine);
    const equityLines = accounts.filter((a) => a.type === 'equity').map(toLine);

    const totalAssets = assetLines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.balance, 0);
    const totalLiabilities = liabilityLines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.balance, 0);
    const totalEquity = equityLines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.balance, 0);
    const currentEarnings = incomeStatement.netIncome;

    return {
      tenantId,
      asOfDate,
      fiscalPeriodId: input.fiscalPeriodId,
      assetLines,
      liabilityLines,
      equityLines,
      totalAssets,
      totalLiabilities,
      totalEquity,
      currentEarnings,
      totalLiabilitiesAndEquity: totalLiabilities + totalEquity + currentEarnings,
      limitationNotice: GL_ONLY_LIMITATION_NOTICE,
    };
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend/src/domain/accounting/services/ChartOfAccountService.ts
============================================================

import { AccountingError } from '../errors.js';
import type { ChartOfAccountRepository } from '../repositories/ChartOfAccountRepository.js';
import type { MappingRepository } from '../repositories/MappingRepository.js';
import type { AccountMapping, CategoryMapping, ChartOfAccount, ChartOfAccountType } from '../types.js';

/** Manages the Accounting Module's own GL chart and its mappings to Cash Module accounts/categories. Never touches the Cash Module. */
export class ChartOfAccountService {
  constructor(
    private readonly chartOfAccounts: ChartOfAccountRepository,
    private readonly mappings: MappingRepository,
  ) {}

  async createChartOfAccount(tenantId: string, input: { code: string; name: string; type: ChartOfAccountType }): Promise<ChartOfAccount> {
    return this.chartOfAccounts.create({
      tenantId,
      code: input.code.trim(),
      name: input.name.trim(),
      type: input.type,
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: new Date(),
    });
  }

  async listChartOfAccounts(tenantId: string): Promise<ChartOfAccount[]> {
    return this.chartOfAccounts.listByTenant(tenantId);
  }

  async setAccountMapping(tenantId: string, cashAccountId: string, chartOfAccountId: string): Promise<AccountMapping> {
    const target = await this.chartOfAccounts.findById(tenantId, chartOfAccountId);
    if (!target) throw new AccountingError('CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND', '找不到這個會計科目。');

    return this.mappings.createAccountMapping({ tenantId, cashAccountId, chartOfAccountId, createdAt: new Date() });
  }

  async setCategoryMapping(tenantId: string, cashCategoryName: string, chartOfAccountId: string): Promise<CategoryMapping> {
    const target = await this.chartOfAccounts.findById(tenantId, chartOfAccountId);
    if (!target) throw new AccountingError('CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND', '找不到這個會計科目。');

    return this.mappings.createCategoryMapping({ tenantId, cashCategoryName: cashCategoryName.trim(), chartOfAccountId, createdAt: new Date() });
  }

  async listAccountMappings(tenantId: string): Promise<AccountMapping[]> {
    return this.mappings.listAccountMappings(tenantId);
  }

  async listCategoryMappings(tenantId: string): Promise<CategoryMapping[]> {
    return this.mappings.listCategoryMappings(tenantId);
  }

  /**
   * The SINGLE shared enforcement point for "an inactive ChartOfAccount
   * cannot receive new postings" (v0.4). Used by BOTH the Cash-mapping
   * posting path (JournalEntryService.journalizeEntry/processPending) and
   * the Manual-voucher posting path (GeneralVoucherService.postDraft) — no
   * other code should duplicate this check.
   */
  async getActiveOwnedAccount(tenantId: string, chartOfAccountId: string): Promise<ChartOfAccount> {
    const account = await this.chartOfAccounts.findById(tenantId, chartOfAccountId);
    if (!account) throw new AccountingError('CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND', '找不到這個會計科目。');
    if (account.status !== 'active') {
      throw new AccountingError('CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_INACTIVE', '此會計科目已停用，無法用於新增分錄。');
    }
    return account;
  }

  /** Activates/deactivates a ChartOfAccount. Deactivating does not touch any existing JournalEntry/JournalLine — it only blocks NEW postings via getActiveOwnedAccount above. */
  async setChartOfAccountActive(tenantId: string, id: string, active: boolean): Promise<ChartOfAccount> {
    const account = await this.chartOfAccounts.findById(tenantId, id);
    if (!account) throw new AccountingError('CHART_OF_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND', '找不到這個會計科目。');
    return this.chartOfAccounts.update({ ...account, status: active ? 'active' : 'inactive' });
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: backend/src/domain/accounting/services/FiscalPeriodService.ts
============================================================

import { AccountingError } from '../errors.js';
import type { FiscalPeriodRepository } from '../repositories/FiscalPeriodRepository.js';
import type { FiscalPeriod } from '../types.js';

export interface CreateFiscalPeriodInput {
  name: string;
  startDate: string; // ISO date, inclusive
  endDate: string; // ISO date, inclusive
}

/**
 * Fiscal Period management (v0.3) — create/list/close only, no reopen.
 * Periods partition time per tenant: creating one rejects any date-range
 * overlap with an existing period of the SAME tenant, but identical ranges
 * across different tenants are unrelated and both allowed (tenant
 * isolation). Closing is one-directional and purely a lock flag — it never
 * posts a JournalEntry, never touches GL data. See
 * docs/architecture/accounting-module-v0.3.md.
 */
export class FiscalPeriodService {
  constructor(private readonly fiscalPeriods: FiscalPeriodRepository) {}

  async createFiscalPeriod(tenantId: string, input: CreateFiscalPeriodInput): Promise<FiscalPeriod> {
    if (input.startDate > input.endDate) {
      throw new AccountingError('FISCAL_PERIOD_INVALID_RANGE', '會計期間的結束日不可早於起始日。');
    }

    const existing = await this.fiscalPeriods.listByTenant(tenantId);
    const overlaps = existing.some((p) => p.startDate <= input.endDate && input.startDate <= p.endDate);
    if (overlaps) {
      throw new AccountingError('FISCAL_PERIOD_OVERLAPS', '此日期區間與現有會計期間重疊。');
    }

    return this.fiscalPeriods.create({
      tenantId,
      name: input.name,
      startDate: input.startDate,
      endDate: input.endDate,
      status: 'open',
      createdAt: new Date(),
    });
  }

  async listFiscalPeriods(tenantId: string): Promise<FiscalPeriod[]> {
    return this.fiscalPeriods.listByTenant(tenantId);
  }

  async getFiscalPeriod(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<FiscalPeriod> {
    const period = await this.fiscalPeriods.findById(tenantId, id);
    if (!period) throw new AccountingError('FISCAL_PERIOD_NOT_FOUND', '找不到指定的會計期間。');
    return period;
  }

  /** One-way close: sets status='closed' + closedAt. Throws if already closed rather than silently no-op-ing, so callers never observe two different "already closed" states. No reopen in v0.3. */
  async closePeriod(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<FiscalPeriod> {
    const period = await this.getFiscalPeriod(tenantId, id);
    if (period.status === 'closed') {
      throw new AccountingError('FISCAL_PERIOD_ALREADY_CLOSED', '此會計期間已經關帳。');
    }
    return this.fiscalPeriods.update({ ...period, status: 'closed', closedAt: new Date() });
  }

  /** Used by JournalEntryService to enforce the closed-period posting lock. Inclusive boundary: startDate <= date <= endDate. A date outside every defined period is never considered closed. */
  async isDateInClosedPeriod(tenantId: string, date: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const periods = await this.fiscalPeriods.listByTenant(tenantId);
    return periods.some((p) => p.status === 'closed' && p.startDate <= date && date <= p.endDate);
  }
}

FILE: backend/src/domain/accounting/services/GeneralVoucherService.ts
